Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cycles variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 424 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 224 and intersection form of rank 1.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in cycles theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 577 and the invariant I(A,B) = 720 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 577 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cycles structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 720. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
